Spiko EU T-Bills Money Market Fund
Spiko EU T-Bills Money Market Fund
by Spiko

Non-U.S. Government Debt
Spiko EU T-Bills Money Market Fund is a short-term variable net asset value money market fund (short-term VNAV MMF) under EU law and a sub-fund of the Spiko SICAV. The Fund invests 100% of its total assets in Treasury Bills issued by investment grade Eurozone Member States, repurchase agreements secured by such securities and cash. The Fund keeps an average maturity of its portfolio under 60 days. The maximum maturity of any asset held by the Fund is less than 6 months. The cash holdings cannot exceed 10% of its total assets.

U.S. Treasuries
Janus Henderson Anemoy Treasury Fund is a tokenized British Virgin Islands (BVI) professional fund, licensed by the British Virgin Islands Financial Services Commission (FSC), and open to non-US Professional Investors. Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund (EUR)
Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund (EUR)
by Spiko

Active Strategies
eurSAFO is the EUR share class of the Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund, a tokenized UCITS fund (French SICAV) co-created by Spiko and Amundi. It generates stable yields above the EUR Short-Term Rate (€STR) benchmark through fully collateralized total return swaps (TRS) with Tier 1 bank counterparties (G-SIBs rated A- or above). The fund holds a diversified portfolio of equities and bonds, and exchanges their total return for overnight rate + spread via daily-reset TRS. This results in a low-volatility, cash-equivalent profile with overnight liquidity. NAV is computed daily by CACEIS and published on-chain via Chainlink oracles. Shares are registered as tokens on Ethereum, Polygon, Arbitrum, Base, Starknet, Etherlink, and Stellar.

PRIME

Asset-Backed Credit
PRIME is a tokenized deposit into Democratized Prime, a warehouse lending facility for HELOCs originated by Figure Technologies (NASDAQ: FIGR). reUSD
reUSD
by Re

Stablecoins
Re Protocol is a decentralized reinsurance platform that connects on-chain capital to real-world insurance opportunities. Participants deposit stablecoins into the protocol’s Insurance Capital Layer and receive tokenized positions: reUSD is a principal‑protected, yield‑accruing token that tracks the greater of (i) the risk‑free rate plus 250 bps or (ii) the Ethena basis‑trade yield plus 250 bps. It earns on‑chain, deploys off‑chain via Surplus Notes to supply regulatory capital, and maintains transparent, oracle‑verified collateral reporting.

Spiko US T-Bills Money Market Fund
Spiko US T-Bills Money Market Fund
by Spiko

U.S. Treasuries
Spiko US T-Bills Money Market Fund is a short-term variable net asset value money market fund (short-term VNAV MMF) under EU law and a sub-fund of the Spiko SICAV. The Fund invests 100% of its total assets in U.S. Treasury Bills (T-Bills), repurchase agreements secured by such securities and cash. The Fund keeps an average maturity of its portfolio under 60 days. The maximum maturity of any asset held by the Fund is less than 6 months. The cash holdings cannot exceed 10% of its total assets.

Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund
Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund
by Spiko

Active Strategies
SAFO is a tokenized UCITS fund (French SICAV) co-created by Spiko and Amundi. It generates stable yields above the SOFR benchmark through fully collateralized total return swaps (TRS) with Tier 1 bank counterparties (G-SIBs rated A- or above). The fund holds a diversified portfolio of equities and bonds, and exchanges their total return for overnight rate + spread via daily-reset TRS. This results in a low-volatility, cash-equivalent profile with overnight liquidity. NAV is computed daily by CACEIS and published on-chain via Chainlink oracles. Shares are registered as tokens on Ethereum, Polygon, Arbitrum, Base, Starknet, Etherlink, and Stellar.

OpenEden OpenDollar
OpenEden OpenDollar
by OpenEden Digital

Stablecoins
The OpenEden OpenDollar ("USDO") is a rebasing yield-bearing stablecoin issued by OpenEden Digital ("OED"), a Bermuda Monetary Authority ("BMA") licensed digital asset issuer. OED is a wholly owned subsidiary of OpenEden Group ("OEG"). The value of USDO is fixed at $1, providing stability to its holders while offering a yield on the underlying reserve assets. USDO rebases daily, which allows holders to earn yield generated from reserves backed by U.S. Treasury bills. This unique mechanism ensures that USDO maintains a stable value while distributing yield to token holders.

REENTAL - LA PAZ 1
REENTAL - LA PAZ 1
by Reental

Real Estate
REENTAL – La Paz 1 (PAZ-1) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in La Paz, Baja California Sur, Mexico, structured as a pre-development tourism and residential project in partnership with M2Capital.The investment model is structured through the RNT-PAZ-1 Series, where MDOS Capital contributes the land lots to a Mexican land trust (fideicomiso) that safeguards the project’s assets. This structure provides asset-backed support to the financing without granting direct ownership of the underlying properties to investors. The project has an expected duration of 5 years, with monthly profit distributions, targeting an estimated 17% total estimated return and 55% annualized estimated return, according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund (GBP)
by Spiko

Active Strategies
gbpSAFO is the GBP share class of the Spiko Amundi Overnight Swap Fund, a tokenized UCITS fund (French SICAV) co-created by Spiko and Amundi. It generates stable yields above the GBP overnight rate (SONIA) benchmark through fully collateralized total return swaps (TRS) with Tier 1 bank counterparties (G-SIBs rated A- or above). The fund holds a diversified portfolio of equities and bonds, and exchanges their total return for overnight rate + spread via daily-reset TRS. This results in a low-volatility, cash-equivalent profile with overnight liquidity. NAV is computed daily by CACEIS and published on-chain via oracle. Shares are registered as tokens on Ethereum, Polygon, Arbitrum, Base, Starknet, Etherlink, and Stellar.

USR
USR
by Resolv

Stablecoins
USR is an overcollateralized, delta-neutral stablecoin. It can be minted and redeemed 1:1 against USD stablecoins (USDC, USDT). The collateral pool behind USR consists of ETH, BTC, and stablecoins, managed through delta-neutral strategies and DeFi strategies. Risk exposures from these strategies are isolated into a separate protection layer represented by the Resolv Liquidity Pool (RLP). USR itself does not accrue yield; users can stake into stUSR to access yield from the underlying strategies.

Quantoz EURQ
Quantoz EURQ
by Quantoz Payments

Stablecoins
The Quantoz EURQ is a euro-pegged stablecoin issued as an ERC-20 token on Ethereum by Quantoz Payments BV, a licensed Electronic Money Institution regulated by the Dutch Central Bank (DNB). Designed to be MiCAR-compliant, its whitepaper has been submitted as an Electronic Money Token (EMT) to the Dutch Financial Authorities (AFM). Distributed via authorized brokers to crypto exchanges, EURQ aims to provide a secure, regulated euro-denominated token for trading and liquidity in European markets. It is 102% backed by euro reserves held by Stichting Quantoz, a bankruptcy-remote entity, across systemically important banks and highly liquid euro bonds, under DNB supervision.

Quantoz USDQ
by Quantoz Payments

Stablecoins
USDQ is a eurozone-regulated, US dollar-pegged stablecoin issued by Quantoz Payments BV, an Electronic Money Institution licensed by the Dutch Central Bank (DNB). Designed for MiCAR compliance, USDQ operates as an ERC-20 token on Ethereum and is distributed through authorized brokers to crypto exchanges. GoldZip Gold
GoldZip Gold
by Midas Labs

Commodities
GoldZip operates in Singapore by GoldZip Digital Pte. Ltd., a group member company of the Hong Kong Gold Exchange and is regulated by the Ministry of Law (MinLaw) in Singapore. Each GoldZip token is backed by 1 gram of 99.99% physical fine gold stored in HKGX recognised and approved gold vaults. Holders of GoldZip token may redeem GoldZip Eligible Gold Bars upon complying with AML requirements. Hong Kong Gold Exchange (HKGX) is Hong Kong’s sole spot gold and silver exchange. It was established through the corporatized restructuring of the Chinese Gold& Silver Exchange Society(CGSE), a historic institution with over 115 years of legacy. Officially launched on January 1, 2025, HKGX marks a new chapter in Hong Kong's precious metals industry. HKGX is currently formed by 143 exchange shareholder members, and offers comprehensive facilities, services and trading platform for participants in precious metals trading. The Exchange has experienced significant growth, with daily trading volumes reaching hundreds of billions of Hong Kong dollars.

Etherfuse CETES
by Etherfuse

Non-U.S. Government Debt
Etherfuse CETES, officially known as Certificados de la Tesorería de la Federación, represent short-term government securities issued by Mexico’s treasury. They act like digital cash equivalents with defined maturity dates and predictable cash flows. In a tokenized form, you get exposure to risk-free rate instruments from one of the larger emerging market economies. They settle quickly and are typically used for capital preservation and yield accrual. Mexican Treasury Bills (CETES) carry a sovereign credit rating of Baa2/BBB, issued by the government of Mexico.

REENTAL - COSTA MEDITERRANEA 1
REENTAL - COSTA MEDITERRANEA 1
by Reental

Real Estate
REENTAL – Costa Mediterránea 1 (CME-1) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in Garrucha, Almería (Spain), focused on the reactivation, completion, and enhancement of an existing residential building with approximately 50% of construction already completed. The project involves finalizing the remaining construction, urban planning regularization, and positioning the asset as a functional residential complex with 1-, 2-, and 3-bedroom units designed to meet diverse market demand. The investment aims to maximize absorption and final asset value in a consolidated coastal market. The investment model is structured through the RNT-CME-1 Series, under which a Reental group company acquires the real estate asset directly, resuming construction and managing the development until project completion and sale. Investors do not acquire direct ownership of the underlying property. The project has an expected duration of 1.5 years, with returns paid exclusively at project closure, targeting an estimated 19.20% annualized return and 28.81% total estimated return, according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

The WisdomTree Private Credit and Alternative Income Digital Fund (Ticker: CRDYX; Token Ticker: CRDT) seeks to track the price and yield performance, before fees and expenses, of the Gapstow Liquid Alternative Credit Index (GLACI). Current planning regulations allow for the development of a multi-family residential building, and the project is currently in an early structuring phase focused on technical and administrative consolidation. The investment structure is established through the RNT-LVT-2 Series, which consists of an asset acquired by a company within the Reental group. loAZND
loAZND
by Mu Digital

Corporate Credit
Asia Dollar (“AZND”) is a credit-backed USD yield token supported by high quality Asia government and corporate borrowers. AZND is designed for stability and steady yield. AZND is suitable for users seeking predictable returns while retaining full DeFi composability To earn the AZND share of yield from the underlying Asset Pool, AZND must be locked into the loAZND vault on supported blockchains. Unlocking AZND from the loAZND vault involves a 1-day cooldown period before withdrawal. Yield is accumulated on a weekly basis and distributed evenly throughout the following week. This is done to even out the distribution of rewards and avoid gaming the reward system if rewards were distributed in one lumpsum. Unlocking is subject to a 1-day cooldown period, the initial AZND tokens and additional AZND rewards are available for withdrawal at the end of the cooldown

REENTAL – Costa del Sol 1 (CDS-1) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in Estepona, Málaga (Spain), one of Spain’s most sought-after coastal markets. The project involves a premium modular residential development designed by architect Fran Silvestre, combining contemporary design, sustainability, and high energy efficiency. The location offers strong residential and tourism demand, with close proximity to the beach, Estepona’s city center, and Marbella. The investment model is structured through the RNT-CDS-1 Series as a bridge loan granted to the company that owns the property. The financing is fully secured by the developer through land preparation valued at €4 million, and structured as a bullet loan, with principal and interest paid at project completion. Investors do not acquire direct ownership of the underlying asset. The project has an expected duration of 6 months, with returns paid exclusively at project closure, targeting an estimated 20% annualized return and 10% total estimated return, according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

Current urban planning regulations permit a change of use, allowing the property to be repositioned into a product aligned with current market demand. The development contemplates: 8 studio apartments of approximately 50 m² each Between 200 and 215 storage units A single-floor mixed-use configuration combining residential and storage components The investment is positioned to capitalize on: Strong demand for compact urban housing Structural shortage of storage space in Madrid Efficient commercialization potential in a high-density urban environment The investment is structured through the RNT-MAD-5 Series, consisting of an asset acquired through a joint structure, with 59% held by a company within the Reental group and 41% by the developer. Real Estate
REENTAL – Bacalar 1 (BKL-1) is a tokenized real-estate eco-luxury hospitality investment located in Bacalar, Mexico, representing the first project developed in this strategic location. The project establishes the foundation for a sustainable luxury ecosystem within a privileged natural environment near the Bacalar lagoon, combining accessibility, privacy, and strong long-term tourism potential. Currently in the urbanization phase, the development is positioned as an entry point into the growing boutique hospitality and wellness tourism market in the region. The architectural concept is based on: Ecological integration Organic materials Tropical architecture blending with the natural surroundings Cabins, open spaces, and nature-oriented amenities Immersive and authentic guest experiences The project targets the expanding luxury and wellness tourism segment, offering strong potential for long-term appreciation and value creation in one of Mexico’s fastest-growing tourism destinations. The investment is structured through the RNT-BKL-1 Series, where MDOS Capital contributes the land lots through a Mexican land trust (fideicomiso) designed to safeguard the project’s assets. The project has an estimated 3-year duration, targeting: 48% total estimated return (ROI) 16% annualized estimated return (IRR) Returns are expected to be distributed monthly, beginning approximately between January 2025 and October 2027, according to the project timeline. Key milestones include: Agreement and signing: November 2024 Project progress monitoring: February 2025 End of contract and project completion: November 2027 Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

REENTAL – Valencia 12 (VLC-12) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in Serra, Valencia (Spain), approximately 25 km from the city of Valencia. USDM1
USDM1
by M1X Global

U.S. Treasuries
USDM1 is a sovereign bond natively issued on-chain by the Republic of the Marshall Islands under New York law. Each unit is backed 1:1 by short-duration U.S. Treasuries in a bankruptcy-remote structure, with holders holding a perfected first-priority security interest. Structured by Cleary Gottlieb as a fully collateralized Brady bond with waiver of sovereign immunity, it features enforceable par redemption and a U.S.-based trustee/collateral agent stack. Compatible with ISDA, GMRA, and GMSLA frameworks, USDM1 supports treatment as sovereign debt or cash equivalent.

The development integrates organic architecture with museums, thematic schools, clubhouses, and curated natural environments, positioning the project within the fast-growing wellness tourism segment with strong long-term appreciation potential. The investment model is structured through the RNT-YTC-1 Series, under which MDOS Capital contributes the land lots to a Mexican land trust (fideicomiso) that safeguards the project’s assets. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

REENTAL - INDEX 1
REENTAL - INDEX 1
by Reental

Real Estate
REENTAL – Index 1 (IDX-1) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment structured as a fully managed, globally diversified portfolio across Spain, Mexico, the United States, and Argentina. The project is designed to provide investors with stable, predictable income by allocating capital across selected high-quality assets, optimizing performance while reducing exposure to individual-property risk. Investors receive a fixed annual return of 12% without needing to select properties or engage in operational management. The investment model operates through the RNT-IDX-1 Series, backed by guarantees on the properties selected for the project. This structure allows investors to benefit from asset-secured exposure without obtaining direct ownership rights over the underlying real estate. The project has a 48-month duration, with monthly return payments, targeting a total estimated return of 48% according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

Etherfuse TESOURO
Etherfuse TESOURO
by Etherfuse

Non-U.S. Government Debt
Etherfuse TESOURO, officially known as Brazilian Letras do Tesouro Nacional, are Brazil’s short-term sovereign bonds. They come in different maturities and structures, including fixed-rate and inflation-indexed ones. Tokenized TESOUROs let you hold a piece of Brazil’s government debt with transparency and digital settlement. They offer a yield that reflects Brazilian interest rate dynamics and inflation expectations. Brazilian Government Bonds (Tesouros) carry a sovereign credit rating of Ba1/BB, issued by the government of Brazil.

REENTAL – Madrid 3 (MAD-3) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in Alcorcón, Madrid (Spain), focused on the acquisition, refurbishment, and sale of a residential asset within one of the fastest-growing municipalities in Madrid’s southern metropolitan area. Real Estate
REENTAL – Bacalar 3 (BKL-3) is a tokenized real-estate eco-luxury hospitality investment located in Bacalar, Mexico, within a privileged natural environment connected to the Bacalar lagoon. It represents the third project developed in this location, building on prior successful developments and reinforcing Reental’s strategic positioning in the region. The project is currently in the urbanization phase and is designed for boutique tourism and wellness-oriented accommodations. The architectural concept emphasizes: Organic materials Integrated natural design Cabins and open terraces Premium amenities such as pools and leisure spaces Immersive nature-driven guest experiences The investment targets the growing luxury and wellness tourism segment, with projected high occupancy potential and long-term value creation. The investment is structured through the RNT-BKL-3 Series, where MDOS Capital contributes the land lots through a Mexican land trust (fideicomiso) designed to safeguard the project’s assets. The project has an estimated 3-year duration, with projected: 48% total estimated return (ROI) 16% annualized estimated return (IRR) Returns are expected to be distributed monthly, beginning approximately between June 2026 and February 2028, according to the project timeline. Additional project milestones include: Agreement and signing: March 2025 Project progress monitoring: April 2025 End of contract and project completion: March 2028 Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

EURe
EURe
by Monerium

Stablecoins
EURe is an e-money token (EMT) issued by Monerium that represents the value of the euro and is issued at par value upon receipt of funds. Each issued token is backed 1:1 by the equivalent amount denominated in euros, safeguarded either as deposits in segregated accounts with credit institutions or invested in qualifying high-quality liquid assets (HQLA) denominated in the same currency. EURe is deployed on multiple blockchain networks (including Ethereum, Gnosis, Arbitrum, Linea, Scroll, Noble, and Polygon) and is designed to enable fast, low-cost onchain euro transfers and integration with SEPA via Monerium’s web3 IBAN.

REENTAL - BRIDGEPORT 1
REENTAL - BRIDGEPORT 1
by Reental

Real Estate
REENTAL – Bridgeport 1 (BPT-1) is a tokenized real-estate loan investment located in Bridgeport, Connecticut (USA), focused on the rehabilitation and conversion of an industrial building into a premium 10-unit multifamily residential property. The project targets a high-demand rental market supported by strong connectivity within Fairfield County and proximity to New York City, benefiting from Bridgeport’s ongoing urban revitalization and waterfront location. The investment model is structured through the RNT-BPT-1 Series and is secured by collateral over the refurbished asset, providing asset-backed protection to the financing. The structure does not grant investors direct ownership of the underlying property, while risk is partially mitigated through a mortgage-backed guarantee. The project has an expected duration of 12 months, with returns paid exclusively at project closure, targeting an estimated 15% annualized return and 15% total estimated return, according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

Etherfuse KTB
Etherfuse KTB
by Etherfuse

Non-U.S. Government Debt
Etherfuse KTB, officially known as Korean Treasury Bonds, represent short-term government securities issued by the Republic of South Korea's Ministry of Economy and Finance. They act like digital cash equivalents with defined maturity dates and predictable cash flows. In a tokenized form, you get exposure to risk-free rate instruments from one of Asia's most advanced and stable economies. They settle quickly and are typically used for capital preservation, currency diversification, and yield accrual. Korean Treasury Bonds carry a sovereign credit rating of AA, issued by the government of South Korea.

The project forms part of the WA Diamond masterplan, a 59,000 m² mixed-use development integrating a corporate campus, lifestyle retail center, multipurpose studios, AAA-grade offices, tourist apartments, and a wellness hub anchored by an international 5-star hotel. The location benefits from strong residential, tourism, and professional demand supported by Salta’s quality of life and expanding infrastructure. The project has an expected duration of 7.5 months, with returns paid exclusively at project closure, targeting an estimated 15.53% annualized return and 9.75% total estimated return, according to the project documentation. Benefits are distributed through the Reental platform.

cSigma USD
cSigma USD
by cSigma Finance

Corporate Credit
csUSD is a yield-bearing ERC-20 token that provides access to institutional private credit yields. Backed by a diversified portfolio of business loans to vetted institutional borrowers across the US, EU, and Asia, csUSD delivers crypto-uncorrelated returns through price appreciation rather than rebasing or token inflation. Built on the ERC-7575 multi-asset vault standard, csUSD aggregates USDC and USDT deposits into a professionally managed credit portfolio. Each token represents a growing claim on the underlying asset pool, creating auto-compounding returns as loan repayments accrue.
